Critical dates shall be those displayed on the procurement portal. Amendments and corrigenda issued before bid closing are part of the tender.

===== PAGE 3 =====
SECTION II - NOTICE INVITING TENDER
1. Invitation
NPIA invites bids for design finalization, supply, installation, integration, testing, commissioning and five-year operations and maintenance of secure network infrastructure across 24 government training institutes.
2. Pre-bid and Clarifications
• Clarification questions shall be submitted in the prescribed window.
• Technical responses to approved clarifications shall form part of the tender where issued as corrigenda.
• Bidders shall review the latest version of the tender before submitting a bid.
3. Bid Submission
• Technical and financial documents shall be submitted in the prescribed bid structure.
• Mandatory forms shall be completed and signed/digitally signed.
• All deviations from minimum technical parameters shall be declared explicitly.
4. Procuring Entity Rights
NPIA may accept or reject bids and may amend or cancel the procurement in accordance with the tender and applicable procurement conditions.

General bidder obligations
• Bidder shall provide technically coherent equipment combinations and identify manufacturer/model for major line items.
• All network configurations shall comply with the required security baseline.
• The bidder shall maintain records necessary for support, asset tracking and audit of configuration changes.
• The bidder shall disclose any subcontracted installation or specialist support scope.
Conflict of interest and eligibility from specified countries shall be handled in accordance with the tender declarations and applicable portal conditions.

===== PAGE 8 =====
SECTION VI - SCOPE OF WORK
• Detailed site survey and finalization of implementation plan.
• Supply and installation of firewalls, managed switches, Wi-Fi 6 access points, racks, power conditioning and network management components.
• Configuration of VLANs, access controls, baseline firewall policies, logging and monitoring.
• Integration of network management system and event retention capability.
• Testing, commissioning and site acceptance.
• Five-year operations and maintenance including preventive maintenance, incident handling, configuration backup and reporting.
• As-built network documentation, device inventory and handover of administrator credentials under the contract procedure.
• Training for designated network administrators.
The bidder shall provide a single coherent implementation responsibility for the awarded package. Equipment interoperability is a bidder responsibility unless a dependency is explicitly stated by NPIA.

===== PAGE 15 =====
SECTION XII - COMMERCIAL AND CONTRACT CONDITIONS
• Performance security: 5% of contract value.
• Payment shall be linked to supply, installation, acceptance and O&M; milestones.
• All required software subscriptions for the contracted service period shall be included in the bid price unless expressly excluded.
• The bidder shall maintain spares/repair arrangements appropriate to the service levels.
• Any subcontracting shall be approved by NPIA and shall not dilute the bidder's responsibility.
